# Breaking P1030680 (U534 — 1 May 1945, M-Thetis)

Target: unbroken Kriegsmarine **Enigma M4** message preserved among the [Hörenberg / U534](https://enigma.hoerenberg.com/index.php?cat=Unbroken&page=P1030680) radio/cipher papers. **Whether U-534 was the intended recipient is open** (Phase 55); recovery provenance is not recipient proof.

| Field | Value |
|-------|--------|
| Indicators | `VROL NMKA` |
| Ciphertext (no indicators) | `JCRSA…HVGF` (72 letters) |
| Key-net | Working assignment **M-Thetis** — `ACH` maps to Thetis in the later Zuteilungsliste used by Hörenberg. A reported earlier `Forelle` list maps column 645 to Aegir; changing allocation lists can reconcile this, but the later list's exact date/codename/provenance is pending (Phase 55). **1 May Kenngruppen Tafel is A under Kennwort Quelle** (Tauschtafelplan Bruno, Prüfnr. 1772a; Phase 61.1). **Tafel A pairs are photographed** in Doppelbuchstabentauschtafeln Prüfnr. **2499** (Phase 61.3). Edition compatibility 1772a vs 2499 is **not** demonstrated — not a dated Zuteilungsliste |
| Intended recipient | **unknown** — preserved among U-534 papers; an intercepted training-network signal is now an OPEN hypothesis, not a fact. NID 1/PW/REP/17 (April 1945) W/T remarks, source-reported from POW and not independently confirmed, make a radio-watch snapshot more plausible than a “messages for U-534” file; they do **not** identify Thetis (Phase 61) |
| Verfahrenkenngruppe | `SEDM` (`OEDM` on the garbled `VA` reading of the trailing repeat) |
| Grundstellung | **unknown** — read from a printed Grund table that no longer exists |
| Not | Potsdam/Plaice — eliminated by exhaustion, see below |

This file is both the attack plan and the **campaign journal**. Negative results are recorded with the same care as positives: what was tried, at what scope, what it ruled out, and where the artifact lives. August 2025–2026 work in HELUT.

**Campaign team.** **Selm Merel Wenselaers** (Historian / Curator, Amsterdam / Antwerp) is now a deeply valued contributor and member of the campaign team, leading the historical/archival arm alongside HELUT's mechanical cryptanalysis. Her source work finds records, reconstructs operational context, and changes which hypotheses are worth testing; HELUT's graded machinery turns those hypotheses into controlled measurements. The findings are joint campaign work, with provenance printed so each result remains reproducible rather than to divide credit. Before the next GPU tranche starts, we report the completed findings and exact planned scope back to her — collaboration is a campaign gate, not an acknowledgement added after the fact.

---

Hard facts from exhaustion and graded controls — not hunches.

Settles *which* Tauschtafel letter P1030680 uses. **Tafel A pair contents** are now photographed in Crypto Museum DoppelTafeln_2499.pdf pp. 3–4 (Prüfnr. **2499**, Kennwort Quelle; Phase 61.3): `VR→ES OL→AE NM→CD KA→HM` and the control maps `FN→KY HC→DM GV→UU ET→ZZ`. No keyboard walks, no repeats, no reuse. There is no operator-habit prior to exploit on this net (Phase 56.5) |
| Indicator / Grundstellung attack | **Mathematically dead, pre-existing result confirmed.** `Scripts/indicator_constraints.py`: message key = f(daily key, Grund), and Grund → message key is a bijection, so trading an unknown message key for an unknown Grund reduces the space by exactly nothing. The 48 (indicator, message-key) pairs cannot be leveraged into the target (Phase 56.5) |
| Cribs from the wider BGNC corpus | **Mechanically strong, historically wrong net — not run, and should not be.** The 4 naval-register additions yield 276,134 legal placements collapsing to **810** distinct (source, diagonal) hypotheses (341×), of which **480** are single-component with loops ≥ 8 and **405** with loops ≥ 12, topping out at **20 loops** — as strong as the FLOTT menus. But the provenance kills them: Graf Spee is **1939** (`GRAFSPEEVONSEEKRIEGSLTG`, `DEVONSHIRE`, `COLBERT`), PAGE_49_TCQB is a Swedish-intercepted lightship position report, and Rasch is U-boat provisioning from Nov/Dec. None is M-Thetis, 1 May 1945, or Baltic training register. Running them would repeat the campaign's most-tested error — the **imported-register wedge**, negative in every arm to date. A 20-loop menu testing the wrong hypothesis is only efficiently wrong (Phase 56.5) |
| Unicity: 72 letters **does** determine the plaintext | **Arithmetic, and it reframes every negative above.** Key entropy ≈ 4.151×10¹¹ settings × 1.507×10¹⁴ ten-plug boards ≈ 6.3×10²⁵ ≈ **2⁸⁶**. German redundancy ≈3.2 bits/letter ⇒ 72 letters ≈ **230 bits**, unicity distance ≈ **27 letters**. So exactly one key yields German and we sit at ~2.7× unicity: every failure to date is a **search/estimator** failure, not a shortage of information. Corollary that sets priority: the plugboard is a **daily** setting, so a second Thetis signal constrains the same 47-bit board with 144 letters instead of 72, and 4 correct plugs already flip the margin positive (Phase 56.2) |
| Independently attested anchors can share **one** menu without inventing a sentence | **Mechanism landed, priced, RUN — see the row above.** A *constellation* unions only the absolute `(step, plain, cipher)` constraints of two separate anchors; no plaintext is asserted in the gap. `FFFTTTBLEIBTBESETZT` × `NEUSTADT` yields **24 single-component rows, 27 edges / 19–21 letters / 7–9 loops**, cross-checked against an independent union-find. This is how the 8-letter `NEUSTADT` contributes at all — the ledger already forbids sweeping it solo. Preflight: 34 944 shells/menu → 1.6×10¹⁰ settings each, 3.83×10¹¹ total, **~2.1 h ETA floor / ~2.8 h at the measured 38M/s** (Phase 56) |
| Constellations buy loops but **widen the span**, and span is what pinned-A misses | **Measured by the tool's own banner, and it sets the run order.** A 27-edge contiguous crib spans 27 positions; these constellations span **47–67**. With the middle ring pinned A the engine reports **~12% (1 notch) to ~23% (2 notches)** of keys uncovered on a 67-letter span, versus a few percent for a compact crib. Loops are not free: the gap the middle-ring arm exists to close is *larger* for wide-span joint menus, so constellations stay **behind** that arm rather than in front of it (Phase 56) |

**Interpretation:** the message is not “unbreakable hardware.” It is unbroken because (1) Thetis has no archival plaintext partner, (2) Potsdam-shaped and primary Regenbogen/Hannibal wedges are eliminated where tested, (3) stochastic search without a near-true template only climbs the coincidence wall, and (4) **the ciphertext itself may be mis-transcribed** — exact Welchman BREAK can discard a true shell that would decrypt a corrected garble. More GPU on wrong letters does not invent the plaintext. TensorLUT grades continuous→discrete logic synthesis and stecker search by construction; quarantine escalates soft linguistic near-misses into a locked-shell Stochastic neighborhood. Closing the mock-PBS boolean loop removes the old “Metal tensors are not plaintext” disclaimer for trivial encodings — it does **not** invent Thetis cribs.

---

## Re-validation, 2026-08-15 (second-model pass)

Not new science. Four campaign claims were carrying prose pointers rather than
runnable receipts; the evidence existed on disk and had never been cited. Nothing
in this ledger's findings changed. Recorded here because the ledger is the
operational record and a re-run belongs in it.

| Claim | What was re-run | Outcome |
|-------|-----------------|---------|
| **C2** Welchman path breaks P1030684 | read `logs/control-p1030684-rings.log` | `*** BREAK FOUND ***` confirmed: UKW B / Greek γ / WO IV-III-VIII / rings AAAH / pos VYYN / stecker BQ CH DI EJ GL KP NV OU SZ TY (all 10) · IC 0.064 vs floor 0.055 · tail −2.848 vs German −2.847 · 2 stops in 1.596×10¹⁰ · German plaintext recovered |
| **C3** ≤10-plug / SAT kill chain | three campaign logs | Sieve confirmed in both directions: 2→2→BREAK on the control; 256 928 raw stops → **0** physical on the fuzzed negative; 1.989×10⁸ → 3 → 0 under the head gate |
| **C11** Potsdam/Plaice ≠ P1030680 | `python3 Scripts/kiss_hunt.py Fixtures/u534_corpus.json` and `helut-bombe --exhaust-selftest` | **Reproduced the August sparse-model receipt exactly**: 3 recovered 1 May daily keys, 72 overlays → 8 survivors vs 5.8 expected by chance. Its known-shell rehearsal ranked the true key **223 118 / 456 976** (= 26⁴, top 48.8%), recovered 4/72 letters and 0/10 plugs, and printed `NO BREAK`. Fresh August logs: `logs/kiss-hunt-revalidate-2026-08-15.log`, `logs/exhaust-selftest-revalidate-2026-08-15.log`. The current dense/objective-v2 receipt keeps that rank, reaches 25/72 and 5/10, and still prints `NO BREAK`: `logs/enigma-dependent-rerun-20260907T030037Z/manifest.json` |
| **C12** windowed discriminator | `logs/campaign-catalog-aaaa-headgate.log` | The old "2 219 physical boards" mass collapses to 3 physical → 0 breaks under the ≥16 filter and dual gate; best `FFFTTTBLEIBTBESE@17` IC 0.051 / tail −4.837, both under bar |

**One number corrected.** The Phase 11 write-up below quoted the bombe at
"~40M settings/s". The archived log measures **37.4M/s** mean
(`total raw stops: 2 over 15968569344 settings (37.4M/s mean)`), so the round-up
is now replaced with the measurement. The `50M/s` also in that log is an ETA
floor the driver prints before it has timed anything -- not a result. The claim
sheet never carried a rate figure, so nothing moved there.

**One number qualified.** The blind control is quoted at 361 s. The archived log
reads **427 s** — 361 s was the first run and the log was overwritten by a re-run
after head scoring. This ledger already recorded both (Phase 11, archival detail);
`writeup.tex` now carries the qualification too.

**One number audited after a code change (2026-09-23), and it held.** The Phase 56
discriminator refactor changed `bestPrefix` to enumerate heads from the last covered
index rather than `offset + edgeCount`, and switched the tail excision from a contiguous
crib range to `coveredPlaintextIndices`. That raised the question of whether the **C12**
figure `FFFTTTBLEIBTBESE@17` IC 0.051 / tail −4.837 could move. It does not. Those two
numbers are the **whole-message** `winner.ic` / `winner.tailScore`, not the head-gate
`prefix` values, and for a single-anchor menu the new covered set is byte-identical to the
old contiguous range (`[17…32]`), so the tail is unchanged to `0.000e+00`: recomputed on
the candidate's exact recovered plaintext, old = new = −4.837234, whole-message IC =
0.050861 → 0.051. The "head gate" in the C12 row names the ≥16 dual **filter applied during
the run**, not the provenance of the printed pair. No re-run is owed; the earlier
"could be stale" caveat was overcautious and is withdrawn. Audit was CPU-only against the
archived log, no GPU (the constellation arm held the device).

**What did not change.** No key, no plaintext, no wedge status. P1030680 remains
unbroken, the catalog stays parked at 417 (`--bombe-from 418`), middle ring ≠ A
remains untested, and the garble hypothesis stands. Full audit: `../AUDIT.md`.

## Campaign journal

### 0 — Statistical search (retired)

Before the archival pivot we treated P1030680 as a ciphertext-only hill-climb: Gillogly-style outer shell search, IC/n-gram fitness, hybrid GA over shell + stecker (`--hybrid`, cleartext Metal batch `B=17576`), and a 3-/4-rotor partition hypothesis. On 72 letters with ~47 bits of plugboard freedom the gradients overfit. Control work on known P1030684 showed the same: high scores that were not the message. **Lesson:** statistics cannot *search* this space; they can only *rank* a tiny survivor list. Tracks retired; see bottom of this file. Notes: `evolution-hybridization.md`, `3-wheel-error-hypothesis.md`, `ASIC_CRACKER.md`.

### 1 — Archival first (history cheaper than search)

Scraped the 1 May 1945 U534 corpus (`Scripts/scrape_u534.py` → `Fixtures/u534_corpus.json`): 48 broken M4 messages, one hand-cipher, **one** unbroken Enigma — this message.

| Finding | Result |
|---|---|
| Depth partner | None — sole unbroken Enigma in the scrape |
| Kiss / re-sent plaintext | 72 overlays → 8 survivors ≈ 5.8 expected by chance — no kiss |
| Three recovered daily keys vs P1030680 | All 26⁴ message keys exhausted; noise floor (bigram ≈ −212) |
| Naval indicators as key constraint | Grund table lost → **1.92×** (~0.94 bit); net ID only |
| Thetis key sheet | Training net; BP never worked it; water-soluble sheets — nothing to find |

**What the archives did buy:** register language. `Scripts/crib_mine.py` mined formulaic cribs from the 48 decrypts and every self-encipherment-legal placement in the target → `Fixtures/p1030680_menus.json` (**100 cribs / 2,513 placements**).

### 2 — Welchman diagonal board (Boolean engine)

Built a crib-driven bombe: host `WelchmanDiagonalBoard.swift`, Metal `BombeMetal.swift` (37.4M settings/s measured, GPU↔host **0 lane mismatches**), driver `BombeSweep.swift`.

**Search reductions (Turing-shaped, essential):** Greek never steps → pin Greek ring A (26×); left notch drives nothing → pin left ring A (26×); **676×** shell collapse, verified on `EnigmaM4Machine.step`, and since measured against a non-AAAA true key (Phase 11). Middle and right rings are absorbed into their window positions only while the span is turnover-free. `--bombe-ring-sweep` buys back the **right** ring at 26× — it does **not** cover the middle ring, which stays pinned at A in every run to date. See the coverage ledger, Phase 12.

**Rehearsal (P1030684 known key), board only:** this grades the diagonal board, not the campaign — it is *handed* the true shell and searches 26⁴ positions. The true setting is kept at every crib length; at **16 letters** the only survivor in 26⁴ is the true key; full historical stecker recovered. Crib length is everything. For the campaign path graded blind, see Phase 11.

### 3 — First openings campaigns

| Artifact | Scope | Outcome |
|---|---|---|
| `logs/welchman-openings.log` | 13 openings+partners, rings AAAA, dual confirm | **0 stops** — every menu contradicted |
| `logs/welchman-openings-rings.log` | openings + partners, **26 right rings**, confirm 1 | **12 raw stops**, all on `UUUVIRSIBENNULEINS@0`; decrypts did not even reproduce the crib |
| `logs/campaign-openings-rings.log` | 30 menus (5 openings + 25 partners), full rings, kill chain | 12 stops → **0** valid 10-plug completions; **4.8×10¹¹** settings |

### 4 — The partial-stecker discovery

Apparent “bombe→Enigma translation bug”: survivors failed `hasPrefix(crib)`. Isolated debug (`--debug-survivor`) proved otherwise:

- Stepping matches the bombe trail 18/18
- Known-good key through the same initializer decrypts P1030684 perfectly
- All mismatches land on letters the bombe never pinned

**Cause:** one-letter seed → one connected component. Split menus return half a board. Forensics (`--bombe-inspect`): `UUUVIRSIBENNULEINS@0` has 3 components; `{BSTVZ}` admits **0/26** seeds — the setting is logically dead. All 12 stops had **0** joint ≤10-plug completions (`logs/discriminator-run.log`). Stage 2 (stecker completion across every component) became mandatory.

### 5 — Automated kill chain

Per-menu loop, host-side only (Metal kernel untouched):

0. Skip crib length < 16  
1. Welchman sweep  
2. ≤10-plug completion (inline as GPU drains)  
3. IC ≥ 0.055, then trigram tail > −3.600 — over the whole message **or** over a readable head of ≥16 non-crib letters → else continue; on pass → `BREAK FOUND`

The head reading was added after Phase 11; the rationale and its two regressions are in the attack plan below.

### 6 — Full catalog, rings AAAA — and a false alarm

| `logs/campaign-all-menus.log` | catalog menus 1–627, rings AAAA | Halted at menu **627** with a claimed break |

**False positive (journaled, not a break):**

- Menu: `KOMXADMXUUUBOO@29` (**14 letters**)
- Shell: UKW B / γ / I-III-VI / rings AAAA / pos `IUUY`
- Stecker: `AS BT DE FY GJ IP MO QV RZ UW`
- Tail **−4.070** vs then-threshold **−4.114** (midpoint fluke of **0.044**)
- IC ≈ **0.043** (noise floor)
- Plaintext outside the crib: garbage (`ZWDHUNLI…KOMXADMXUUUBOO…HEQQQPBV…`)
- Cost to that point: ~193M raw stops, 2,118 physical completions

**Hardening that followed:** min crib 16; break bar **−3.600**; IC gate **0.055**; `--bombe-from N` resume. Spec: `discriminator.md`.

**Rings-AAAA verdict for ≥16 menus: clean negative** — 627 + 259 = **886** placements of length ≥16, which is the whole catalog above the filter. Read it narrowly: rings AAAA is exhaustive only over turnover-free spans, which the sweep itself prints as **0–19%** of keys depending on crib length. If the true key is under these cribs, it needs a turnover inside the span, and that is what Phase 8 onward goes after.

### 11 — Blind control: grading the campaign, not the board

The rehearsal was never a test of the campaign. `--welchman-rehearsal` **hands the bombe
the true shell** (γ, IV-III-VIII, UKW B, rings `AACU`) and searches 26⁴ window positions.
The thing that produced every negative above — 336 Walzenlagen × 2 Greek × 2 UKW × 26 right
rings, GPU kernel, stecker completion, discriminator — had never been graded against a key
it was not told.

- `--bombe-fixture <path>` added, so a control runs without swapping the live catalog
- `Scripts/build_control_fixture.py` → `Fixtures/p1030684_control_menus.json`: P1030684
  ciphertext, cribs lifted from the true plaintext at their true offsets, sized to campaign
  scale (27 letters / 27 edges / **8 loops** — weaker connectivity than the top-30 set)

| Artifact | Scope | Outcome |
|---|---|---|
| `logs/control-p1030684-rings.log` | 3 control menus × 26 right rings, blind | **BREAK on menu 1.** First run 361 s; re-run after head scoring 427 s (log overwritten). Same shell, same ten plugs, IC 0.064, tail −2.848 |

```
UKW B / Greek gamma / WO IV-III-VIII / rings AAAH / pos VYYN
stecker BQ CH DI EJ GL KP NV OU SZ TY  (all ten historical plugs)
VVVUUUVIRSOBENNULEINSXXMITUUUVIRSIBENNULZWOYVIRSIBENNULDREIY…PASSIRT
```

**Why `AAAH/VYYN` and not the historical `AACU/VYAA`** — the reduction working exactly as
claimed, which is the second thing this control proves:

- middle: truth ring `C`(2) pos `A`(0) → ring `A` pos `Y`(24) = 0 − 2 mod 26. The absorption
  shift, measured rather than asserted, against a true key that is *not* rings-AAAA
- right: core offset preserved, 0 − 20 ≡ 13 − 7 = 6. Rotor VIII carries **two** notches
  (M and Z, 13 apart), so a 13-shift in (ring, position) is an exact equivalence there

**Graded by this run:** Metal kernel at campaign scale, the 336-WO enumeration, Greek/UKW
enumeration, the right-ring sweep, Greek/left/middle ring pinning, stecker completion
(10/10 plugs), and the discriminator gates — the true key cleared the bar with margin and
was one of only two stops in the whole run.

**Verdict: the negatives are real negatives.** Pointed at a key that is present, this engine
finds it, blind, in six minutes. Nothing above needs re-running for correctness. What the
audit *did* find is that far less has been eliminated than the log lines claimed.

### 12 — Coverage ledger (what is actually eliminated)

Three overclaims, all now fixed in the code that prints them:

1. `right ring swept: turnover phase fully covered, elimination is complete` — false. The
   sweep covers every phase of the *right*-wheel turnover, so middle-wheel stepping is
   right for all 26. The **middle ring stays pinned at A**, which is only exact while the
   middle wheel does not itself turn over in span. A key that steps the left wheel mid-crib
   is out of reach of every run to date — at the 27–40 letter spans in play that is roughly
   **8%** of keys with a one-notch middle wheel and **15%** with a two-notch one (VI–VIII).
2. "all 2,513 placements" — the catalog runs tested the **886** placements of length ≥16.
   The other 1,627 were dropped by the min-crib filter, by design, and never tested.
3. "dead at the board" on the longest menus — partly false. The Metal kernel tabulates one
   upper-involution table per distinct slow-wheel `(l,m)` state and capped that at **four**;
   overflow wrote `outSurvivors[lane] = 0`, i.e. it reported a lane *eliminated* that it had
   never tested. `Scripts/max_upper_audit.py` settles how often that fired: never below 28
   letters, and up to **5.56%** of lanes at 40. See Phase 49.

| Arm | Placements | Ring coverage | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| Exact catalog ≥16, rings AAAA | 868 (head gate) | turnover-free spans only — **0–19%** by crib length | complete — clean negative |
| Exact, right ring swept | **54** curated + catalog through **417**/2513 done; **SUSPENDED** resume `--bombe-from 418` | all 26 | Phase 15
| Fuzzed, rings AAAA | 400 | turnover-free spans only | complete |
| Fuzzed, right ring swept | **40** | all 26 | complete — clean negative (0 raw stops) |
| Placements < 16 letters | 1,627 | none | never tested |
| **Hapax phrases (1 carrier msg)** | — | — | The **2,691,455** legal-placement surface was enumerated 2026-08-16; its first 24-place relay fixture was never run. The three hand-prioritised leads Wenselaers supplied are **DONE: 28/28 dead, 0 raw stops** (Phase 52.3); both edit arms priced but not run (Phase 52.4) |
| Middle ring ≠ A | — | — | **still untested against P1030680**; arm implemented + graded (Phase 49), `--bombe-middle-ring` |
| Long menus (len 28/30/40), any prior arm | 61 spans | — | **incomplete** — up to 5.56% of lanes silently eliminated by the old `MAX_UPPER=4` (Phase 49) |

So: **182 of 886 exact placements (~21%) have had real ring coverage** so far (plus 54
curated). The headline "clean negative on the catalog" was never a negative over the full
ring space. That is the honest state, and it is why the resume is still worth its days.

### 13 — The deeper suspicion: the crib register is imported

The corpus is 48 broken messages, and every one of them belongs to Potsdam (31), the second
net (16) or the third net (1). **None is M-Thetis.** P1030680 is the sole Thetis message in
the scrape — that is the whole reason it is unbroken. So every crib driving every campaign
above is register mined from *other nets' traffic*, and Thetis was a training net that
Bletchley never worked.

If Thetis openings do not look like Potsdam openings, no amount of ring coverage or
orthographic fuzzing will help, because the wedge itself is wrong. This does not invalidate
the plan below; it sets the order.

| Artifact | Scope | Outcome |
|---|---|---|
| `logs/campaign-catalog-aaaa-headgate.log` | 868 menus ≥16, rings AAAA, head+whole gate | **Clean negative.** 1.99×10⁸ raw stops → **3** physical → **0** breaks. Best: `FFFTTTBLEIBTBESE@17`, IC 0.051 / tail −4.837 (under IC 0.055 and −3.600). 5.33×10¹¹ settings @ 32.7M/s |

The head reading did not resurrect a true key from the turnover-free slice. The old
"2,219 physical boards" mass was almost entirely short-crib / weak-menu ghosts that the
current ≥16 filter and dual gate refuse to score; only three boards cleared the plug sieve,
and none cleared the linguistic bar on either reading.

**Verdict:** exact ≥16 cribs are eliminated under rings AAAA *including* the mid-message
divergence hypothesis. What remains of the exact catalog is turnover coverage
(`--bombe-ring-sweep`).

### 16 — Übung header hypothesis

Thetis was a training net. Live BdU traffic opened with operational address groups;
training traffic was padded with **UEBUNG** (Enigma: no umlaut → `UEBUNG`). If the
plaintext opens `UEBUNGXUEBUNG…`, every Potsdam opening crib is sitting in the wrong
place — pushed to roughly offset 6–20 — and more ring coverage on the old catalog will
not help.

**Cipher fact already known:** `UEBUNGXUEBUNGXUEBUNG` (20) is **illegal at offset 0**
(plain `X` ↔ cipher `X` at position 13). It is legal at offset 1. Self-stecker, not the
bombe, already kills that exact pad at the absolute head.

**What is and is not new surface**

| Arm | Status under rings AAAA |
|---|---|
| Exact ≥16 Potsdam cribs at offsets 10–20 | Already in the catalog (~163 placements). Head-gate AAAA cleared them. Shifting alone is mostly done. |
| Thetis header vocabulary (`UEBUNG` / `FUNKUEBUNG` / pads) ≥16 at 0–2 | **Arm 1 clean negative** — 0 physical |
| Short `UEBUNG` (6–15) alone | Forbidden — menu-627 ghost factory; prefix “clears the bar” flukes held |
| Header@0–2 + independent body@10–20, same shell | **Arm 2 body-side negative** under AAAA — all 40 anchors dead; shorts aborted |

**Ghost-safe protocol (do this order)**

1. **Solo-safe header fixture (≥16 only).** Pad/repeat until length ≥16:
   `UEBUNGUEBUNGUEBUNG`, `UEBUNGXUEBUNGXUEBUNG`, `FUNKUEBUNGFUNKUEBUNG`,
   `UEBUNGSMELDUNGXX`, etc. Offsets **0, 1, 2** only. Keep `--bombe-min-crib 16`
   `--bombe-confirm 1`. Rings AAAA. Tiny run — tens of menus, not hundreds.
2. **If empty: same headers + body partners in one run.** Strong ≥16 operational cribs
   forced to offsets 10–20 (and any catalog mid-placements worth re-pairing). Run with
   `--bombe-confirm 2`. Independence is free when offsets differ.
3. **Before any crib <16 enters the GPU:** change the sweep so `isBreak` cannot fire
   from a short menu unless that shell already has ≥2 independent agreements. Today
   confirm only prints at the end — a solo ghost can still halt the campaign mid-run.
4. **Ring-sweep only** menus/shells that survive (1) or agree in (2). Do not start a
   4-day ring campaign on virgin short headers.

**Arm 1 result** (`logs/campaign-uebung-aaaa.log`): **Clean negative.** 46 placements,
2.83×10¹⁰ settings @ 44.3M/s, 5,614 raw stops → **0** physical 10-plug boards. Strong
pads (`UEBUNG…UEBUNG`, `FUNKUEBUNG…`) died at the board; weaker `…MELDUNG…` tails
produced only ghosts. These exact ≥16 training headers are not in the message at
offsets 0–2 under rings AAAA.

Next: arm 2 — short UEBUNG (6–14) @0–2 + top mid-message body cribs @10–20,
`--bombe-confirm 2 --bombe-min-crib 6`. Solo BREAK gated: cribs <16 cannot claim
a break without independent shell agreement. Fixture:
`Fixtures/p1030680_uebung_pair_menus.json`.

**Arm 2 partial** (`logs/campaign-uebung-pair-aaaa.log`, aborted ~2 h into short menus):
All **40** ≥16 body anchors died at the board under rings AAAA — so confirm-2 was
already impossible before any short header could help. The short menus then stalled
the host: `FUNKUEBUNG@1` alone cost ~23 min for **40M** raw stops × stecker completion.
Rate fell from ~45M/s to ~7M/s. Several shorts also printed “clears the bar” on
prefix flukes while being held for agreement.

**Ghost killer shipped (does not risk the one key under confirm≥2):** short challengers
no longer GPU-sweep. Anchors (≥16) run first and lock physical shells; challengers
only host-retest those shells. If no anchor locks anything, shorts are skipped.
A true key still survives: its ≥16 body locks the shell, then the short header
confirms it. What dies is the 40M-ghost completion flood.

Arm 2 under AAAA is therefore a **clean negative on the body side** — the Übung
“push the Potsdam crib to offset 10–20” claim is dead in the turnover-free slice.
Remaining excuse: **ring-sweep those body anchors** (`Fixtures/p1030680_uebung_body_menus.json`,
`logs/campaign-uebung-body-rings.log`). If any lock a shell, short UEBUNG headers
re-test them via the anchor-intersection sieve.

**Arm 2 body × rings** (`logs/campaign-uebung-body-rings.log`): **Clean negative.**
40 mid-message ≥16 anchors × 26 right rings, 6.39×10¹¹ settings @ 36.5M/s,
**0** raw stops — every menu dead at the board. Turnover does not revive the
“header pushes Potsdam body to offset 10–20” claim for these cribs. Short-header
confirm has nothing to lock against. The Übung-push hypothesis is exhausted under
right-ring coverage (middle ring still pinned A).

### 18 — Stochastic Bombe (match fitness, not German n-grams)

**Reversing Turing.** The 1940s Bombe asked: given a crib, which keys are
*impossible*? Contradiction killed drums. We can now ask the dual question the
drums could not afford: given a hypothesized plaintext template, which keys are
*better*? GPUs score millions of cleartext decrypts in parallel; a genetic loop
climbs letter-match. That is not inventing plaintext in \(26^{72}\) — it is
searching keys while \(P\) stays fixed (or a small template library). German
n-grams are optional; Thetis need not look like Potsdam. The bigram `--hybrid`
path already failed this control at 72 letters.

Shipped (GPU live):

- Metal `m4_kpa_batch` — cleartext decrypt × letter-match (`B=17 576`/Greek)
- `--hybrid --hybrid-control` — lock P1030684 shell, evolve stecker under KPA
- Parallel: GPU saturates each chromosome; bigram mode still parallelizes the population
- `--hybrid-blind` / `--hybrid-seed-drop N` / elite KPA hill-climb

**Control grades (first 72 of P1030684):**

| Mode | Outcome | Log |
|------|---------|-----|
| Oracle stecker seed | **PASS** 72/72, pos `VYAA`, ~0.2 s | `logs/stochastic-bombe-control-seeded.log` |
| Near-miss drop 1 plug | **PASS** 65→72 via hill-climb, ~2 s | `logs/stochastic-bombe-control-nearmiss-drop1.log` |
| Blind stecker | 40×24: climbed to **22/72**, stuck (local max) — FAIL | `logs/stochastic-bombe-control-blind-climb.log` |

**Do we just evolve more?** No. The datapath works (oracle + near-miss prove it).
Cold-start stecker space is ~\(10^{14}\) with a rugged match landscape: random
boards can hit ~16–22/72 by maximizing over \(26^4\) message keys alone, then
hill-climb parks on that false peak. More generations without a better operator
or a nearer seed will not magically reach 72.

**Rigor campaign** (`--hybrid-stochastic --hybrid-rigor`, 2026-08-10):

Protocol: ratio fitness; survivor iff `ratio ≥ 0.80` **and**
`ratio ≥ noiseFloor + 0.10`; empirical noise from 8 random shells per cell;
structural bank `Fixtures/p1030680_stochastic_structural.json` (21 masks,
16–24 constrained, self-stecker legal); subspaces **potsdam** + **two-notch**;
rings AAAA/AACU; pop 12 × gens 12.

| Result | Value |
|--------|-------|
| Cells | 21 templates × 2 priors = 42 |
| Survivors | **0** |
| Best ratio | **11/16 (68.8%)** `struct:THETIS@1` @ potsdam (noise 44%, Δ +25%) |
| Best two-notch | 10/16 (62.5%) `struct:THETIS@0` |
| Wall clock | 185.6 s |
| Logs | `logs/stochastic-bombe-p1030680-rigor.log`, `.json` |

**Verdict: clean negative under the rigor bar.** Neither the Potsdam-wheel prior
nor the two-notch prior puts any curated Thetis structural template near the
truth. Peaks in the 55–69% band with positive Δ over noise are still below 80% —
consistent with a searchable landscape that is not yet aimed at the right
plaintext shape. **Do not** resume catalog rings on the strength of these
partials. Next: invent qualitatively new hypotheses (or archival Thetis scraps),
then re-run the same rigor gate.

Notes: `stochastic-bombe.md`. Fixture: `Fixtures/p1030680_stochastic_structural.json`.

### 21 — TensorLUT continuous–discrete compiler (parallel track)

Not a new crib wedge — a greenfield datapath that melts Yosys LUT6 netlists into
learnable 64-wide `Float32` INIT tensors, evolves them under \(F_{crypto}\) + λ binary
penalty, and emits gate-level Verilog. SoftLUT renamed **TensorLUT** end-to-end
(`tensorlut.md`, `adversarial-synthesis.md`, `PRD_tensorlut*.md`).

| Checkpoint | Result |
|---|---|
| Multilinear Metal eval + DFF tick | XOR / Delay-XOR / 2-bit adder cold-start discovery **PASS** |
| Unmutated `enigma_m4` stream vs cleartext | Crib `TEST` @ AAA → \(F_{crypto}=0\) (post-tick sample of registered PT) |
| `TensorLUTEmitter` baseline | `enigma_m4_tensorlut_baseline.v` — **925 LUT6 + 49 DFFs**, ~158 KB; INIT hex round-trip checked |
| M4 full cold-start melt | Wipe **59,200** INITs → `0.5`; explore λ=0 (250) → squeeze λ→10 (500) — **finished, shattered** |
| `freezeMask` + targeted melt | Per-LUT freeze skips mutation / friction; melt-only wipe |
| `TensorLUTConeTagger` | CT-closure + registered-PT unwrap → **16** melt LUTs (not ~700 BFS flood) |
| Stecker-cone INIT melt + resign | Explore −1.90; squeeze still fractional; identity-stecker resign control: **3 RESIGNs** |
| Stecker involution sandwich | Freeze full core; evolve ≤10 reciprocal pairs — identity \(F_{crypto}=0\) **PASS**; blind `AB CD EF` on 14-letter crib full-map **PASS** (~6 min) |

#### Challenges and adaptations

**1. Dependency-chain shatter (full wipe).** Explore climbed \(F_{crypto}\) **−4.59 → −1.22**
(non-binary 58,276 → 30,857). Squeeze λ→~10 snapped weights toward binary (non-binary →
**14,010**) but **regressed crypto to −8.04**. Continuous soft paths that ignore rotor
wiring look good under λ=0 and die under discreteness. Artifact
`enigma_m4_tensorlut_coldstart.v` is historical junk, not rediscovered Enigma.

**2. Targeted melt + freeze mask.** Adaptation: freeze known-good LUT blocks; wipe only the
unknown region. Tiny nets (2-bit adder) rediscover under melt masks — the loop works when
the genotype is the right size.

**3. Cone tagging without hierarchy.** Abc-flattened `enigma_m4` has no `plugboard` cell
names. Naive reverse BFS from CT/PT floods ~700 LUTs (state-polluted cloud). Adaptation:
`TensorLUTConeTagger` — CT-side closure that refuses state-Q wires; PT is registered so
unwrap Q→D then reverse *outside* the polluted cloud. Result: forward
`105–111,113,115,117,119` + reverse `397,399,542,786,902` = **16** melt indices.

**4. INIT melt ≠ stecker.** Melting those 16 still shattered under λ (explore −1.90; squeeze
crypto −4.94 with 118 melt fractions). Early resignation (`resignNonBinaryAbove` at
λ≥½λmax) correctly aborts doomed lineages — baseline identity-stecker control: 3 RESIGNs
at λ≈5 with hundreds of fractional melt weights (`logs/tensorlut-m4-stecker-resign-control.log`).
**Root cause:** Verilog `plugboard()` is identity `clamp26`. The cone LUTs are I/O codecs,
not a 26×26 involution. Independent INIT floats + isolated \(w(1-w)\) never enforce
A↔X reciprocity.

**5. Stecker involution by construction.** Adaptation: freeze the entire known-good TensorLUT
core (identity stecker in silicon). Chromosome = `SteckerInvolution` (≤10 disjoint pairs).
Sandwich: inject `S(CT)` → frozen core → score soft PT vs bits of `S(P)`. The GA cannot
invent non-reciprocal maps. Identity seed on `TEST`/`YCPY` @ AAA → \(F_{crypto}=0\).

**6. Blind rediscovery + sequential plugs.** Short cribs admit false \(F=0\) steckers and leave
unused plugs unconstrained. Adaptation: grade **active-map** match on CT∪PT; **parsimony**;
**`--stecker-grow`** raises the pair budget on plateau; **soft freeze + thaw** keeps elite
plugs as a bias but allows 25% free mutate and clears the freeze on deep plateau (hard freeze
locked a wrong second pair `DP` vs `CD`). Blind control: target `AB CD EF`, crib
`ABCDEFGHIJKLMN`, no oracle seed → gen 60, \(F=0\), full-map **PASS**
(`logs/tensorlut-m4-stecker-involution-blind-3pair.log`, ~6 min).

**CLI:**

```bash
# Locked physical baseline (already written)
./.build/release/helut -- enigma_m4_netlist.json --emit-tensorlut-verilog \
  --emit-out enigma_m4_tensorlut_baseline.v

# Full cold-start (shatter diagnosis — historical)
./.build/release/helut -- enigma_m4_netlist.json --tensorlut-cold-start \
  --cold-log logs/tensorlut-m4-coldstart.log \
  --cold-emit-out enigma_m4_tensorlut_coldstart.v

# Tag / melt stecker cones (INIT path — structural dead end for reciprocity)
./.build/release/helut -- enigma_m4_netlist.json --tag-stecker-cones
./.build/release/helut -- enigma_m4_netlist.json --tensorlut-targeted-melt --melt-stecker \
  --cold-log logs/tensorlut-m4-stecker-melt.log

# Stecker path that respects reciprocity (blind rediscovery)
./.build/release/helut -- enigma_m4_netlist.json --tensorlut-stecker-involution \
  --stecker-blind --stecker-target "AB CD EF" --stecker-grow \
  --cold-crib ABCDEFGHIJKLMN --cold-log logs/tensorlut-m4-stecker-involution-blind-3pair.log
```

Welchman catalog rings and TensorLUT share the GPU; treat this as a parallel research arm,
not a substitute for menu coverage.

### 22 — Near-miss quarantine (garble escalation)

Sister traffic P1030681 (Dönitz message) required [Girard/Hörenberg degarbling](https://enigma.hoerenberg.com/index.php?cat=The%20U534%20messages&page=Degarbling%20the%20D%C3%B6nitz%20Message%20P1030681):
dual Schlüsselzettel vs plain-paper copies, U↔N / Q↔G confusions, a missing four-letter
group. **P1030680 may be similarly dirty.** A true key can therefore fail the strict
Welchman BREAK bar (IC ≥ 0.055 ∧ tail > −3.600) while still sitting in a soft band.

**Pipeline (shipped):**
1. **Quarantine gate** — physical + `cribExact` + *not* `isBreak`, but soft IC ≥ 0.048 and
   soft tail > −4.000 (whole or prefix). Written to `logs/quarantine_candidates.json`
   (disable: `--bombe-no-quarantine`; path: `--bombe-quarantine`).
2. **Escalate** — `--hybrid --hybrid-stochastic --hybrid-quarantine <path>` injects each
   candidate as a full `ShellChromosome` seed; default **locks** WO/Greek/UKW/rings and
   hill-climbs stecker (`--hybrid-quarantine-free-shell` to unlock).
3. **Survivor bar unchanged** — Stochastic still demands ratio ≥ 80% ∩ noise+10%; quarantine
   only changes the *starting neighborhood*.

Sample ingest fixture: `Fixtures/quarantine_candidates.sample.json`.

**Control grade (garbled known cipher):**
```bash
./.build/release/helut --welchman-quarantine-control --garble-flips 20
# Historical Dönitz (Girard/Hörenberg P1030681):
./.build/release/helut --welchman-quarantine-control --garble-source donitz --garble-crib-len 24
./.build/release/helut --welchman-quarantine-control --garble-source donitz-first --garble-crib-len 24
```
P1030684 clean CT → BREAK. Block-wipe 20 CT letters after an 18-letter crib → soft-band
near-miss (not BREAK) → `logs/quarantine_p1030684_garbled.json` → Hybrid KPA escalate
recovers ~100−flips letters (PASS ≥80%). Discriminator provisional IC gate drops to the
soft floor when quarantine is armed, otherwise soft-IC stops were invisible.

P1030681 Schlüsselzettel re-check is only **12** CT diffs — true key still BREAKs (WEAK /
partial PASS); escalate hits edit ceiling 360/372. First draft (aligned `.`→X + insert
`HMHY`) is **38** diffs: soft-band trap (IC 0.054, not BREAK) → escalate **334/372**
(edit ceiling). Logs: `quarantine-donitz-control.log`, `quarantine-donitz-first-control.log`.

**Log audit (prior Welchman arms):** no soft-band hits on P1030680 at the default soft
tail (−4.000). Closest — `UEBUNGXUEBUNG@0` IC 0.049 / tail −4.270 (Δsoft +0.27).

**UEBUNG soft-tail playbook (temporary −4.3):**
```bash
./.build/release/helut --welchman \
  --bombe-fixture Fixtures/p1030680_uebung_nearmiss_menus.json \
  --bombe-menus 0 --bombe-confirm 1 --bombe-min-crib 6 \
  --bombe-quarantine logs/quarantine_candidates.json \
  --bombe-quarantine-tail-margin 0.7
# margin 0.7 → soft tail > −4.300 (breakThreshold −3.600)
./.build/release/helut --hybrid --hybrid-stochastic \
  --hybrid-quarantine logs/quarantine_candidates.json \
  --hybrid-pop 64 --hybrid-gens 24
```
Caught **55** soft-band near-misses (1 whole-band: UKW B / γ / I-IV-VI / AAAA /
`HZBR`, crib `UEBUNGXUEBUNG@0`). Hybrid locked-shell escalate: **0 survivors** under
80% (ceiling ~60% = coincidence). Snapshot:
`logs/quarantine_uebung_nearmiss_soft43.json`,
`logs/campaign-uebung-nearmiss-soft43.log`,
`logs/stochastic-bombe-uebung-quarantine-escalate.{out,err}`.
Default soft floor stays −4.000; −4.3 is CLI-only archaeology.

### 24 — Close the mock-PBS boolean loop (systems, not a break)

HELUT’s Metal path was TFHE-*shaped* but not boolean-safe: `$lut` cells XOR-packed wires
and multiplied by a **random** Toeplitz keyed only by the truth-table *string*, so
`plaintext_char` / `linguistic_score` tensors were not plaintext. Campaign work correctly
routed fitness through host M4 + cleartext Metal batch instead.

**Fix:** trivial torus encoding (bit `b` ↔ constant-fill `{0,1}`) was already what DFF muxes
and the bombe harness used. Replace LUT lowering with the **multilinear expansion** of the
Yosys truth table over $\mathbb{Z}/2^{32}\mathbb{Z}$ — exact on $\{0,1\}^k$, keeps shape
`[B,N]`. Phase-1 dense negacyclic matvec stays as `NegacyclicMatvecNode` (modular kernel
proof only).

| Gate | Result |
|---|---|
| `MockPBSBooleanTests` full adder | Metal ≡ `CleartextNetlistSim` (all 8 rows) |
| Stateful counter 16 ticks | Metal ≡ cleartext |
| Short Enigma netlist stream | Metal ≡ clear harness round-trip |

**Campaign implication:** in-graph decrypt under mock PBS is now meaningful. It does **not**
change P1030680 coverage — the message is still unbroken for archival reasons. Welchman /
Stochastic / quarantine remain the search engines; mock PBS is the FHE datapath loop closed.

### 31 — Crypto-gadget Metal + real KS + packed-GLWE wires (step 10a)

`MetalGGSW` multi-level external product (`β=8`, `ℓ=4`) ≡ CPU. Real
`makeKeySwitchKey` / `keySwitch` (GLev, e=0) between binary secrets.
`--encoding glwe-packed`: Metal wires `mask‖body` (width `2N`); `$lut` slices
body, runs PBS/multilinear, re-packs zero-mask ‖ body. Full adder ≡ cleartext
under all encodings including packed (`TFHESeamTests`).

| Gate | Result |
|---|---|
| Metal crypto-gadget ⋉ ≡ CPU | PASS |
| Real KS between secrets (e=0) | PASS |
| Packed encoding round-trip | PASS |
| Metal full_adder × glwe-packed × {pbs, pbs-ggsw} | PASS |

**Not claimed:** LWE noise; encrypted masks on netlist wires; Metal GGSW as the
netlist `$lut` body (still body-slice CMUX/multilinear).

**Next (step 10b):** discrete noise, encrypted packed GLWE, Metal GGSW `$lut` body.

GGSW CMUX `$lut` chain on Metal (CPU rotate) ≡ `evaluatePBSGGSW` / multilinear.

| Noisy GLWE boolean round-trip | PASS |
| Encrypted packed encode/decode | PASS |
| Metal `evaluatePBS` XOR ≡ CPU | PASS |

**Next (step 10c):** encrypted Yosys netlist via GGSW PBS per `$lut`.

`evaluatePBSGGSWCiphertext` (CPU) or `MetalGGSW.evaluatePBSCiphertext`.
full_adder ≡ cleartext (8/8). CLI: `--bench-encrypted`.

Honest hybrid: **clear selectors** (decrypt wire → GGSW encrypt of the bit);
PBS accumulator / output wire stays encrypted. Combinational modules only.
