## Active architecture

```
UKW thin {B,C} × Greek {β,γ} × P(8,3)=336 Walzenlagen
× Ringstellung (AAAA | --bombe-ring-sweep)
× Stecker ≤10 plugs (SAT + completion)
× Nachrichten-Schlüssel 26⁴
```

**Code:** `WelchmanDiagonalBoard.swift` (Boolean), `BombeMetal.swift` (GPU, locked), `BombeSweep.swift` (driver), `PostBombeDiscriminator.swift` (stages 2–3).

**Break claim:** crib exact ∧ IC ≥ 0.055 ∧ trigram tail > −3.600 — over the whole message, **or** over a head of ≥16 non-crib letters, which is the only shape a mid-message turnover can produce under a pinned middle ring.

**Rehearsal (P1030684), board only:** true shell supplied; at 16 letters, 1 survivor in 26⁴ = true key; discriminator recovers `BQ CH DI EJ GL KP NV OU SZ TY`, pos `VYAA`, IC 0.064, tail −2.889.

**Blind control (P1030684), whole campaign:** nothing supplied; break on menu 1 in 361 s at 27 letters, `AAAH/VYYN` ≡ `AACU/VYAA`, all ten plugs, 2 stops in 1.6×10¹⁰ settings. See Phase 11.

| Path | Rate |
|---|---|
| host | 0.17M settings/s |
| Metal | ~35–50M/s (~40M/s in campaigns) |

```bash
swift run -c release helut --welchman-rehearsal
swift run -c release helut --welchman --bombe-menus 0 --bombe-confirm 1
swift run -c release helut --welchman --bombe-from 628
swift run -c release helut --debug-survivor
swift run -c release helut --bombe-inspect 'B:beta:VII-V-VIII:AAAH:PBMK:CRIB@0'
python3 Scripts/select_top30_menus.py
python3 Scripts/fuzzy_crib_generator.py

# Grade the campaign itself against a known key (Phase 11) — ~6 min to break
python3 Scripts/build_control_fixture.py
swift run -c release helut --welchman \
  --bombe-fixture Fixtures/p1030684_control_menus.json \
  --bombe-menus 3 --bombe-ring-sweep
```

`--bombe-fixture` replaces the old `cp`-the-catalog-and-`trap`-it-back ritual. Any campaign can now be aimed at any menu file without risking `p1030680_menus.json`.

**Near-miss found during the audit:** `Fixtures/p1030680_menus.json.full`, the "safe copy" that every swap ritual restored from, held a byte-identical copy of the **400-menu fuzzed fixture** — not the catalog. Any trap firing would have silently replaced 2,513 placements with 400. The catalog survived only because it was restored from git instead. Deleted; use `--bombe-fixture`.

## Retired tracks

**Phase B — Gillogly-style outer search** — IC + stecker hill-climb per shell. False positives.

**Phase B2 — Crib-dragging** — integrated; output feeds Welchman menus.

**Hybrid GA × 3/4-rotor partition** — retired as full-shell search. Revive only crib-less, shell-constrained, stecker-only — never as an outer GA over Thetis.

```bash
# Do not use for final extraction

```

## Honest scope

- The engine is graded, not trusted: a blind control breaks a known key through the full campaign path in 361 s, all ten plugs (Phase 11). Every negative below is a real negative.
- Potsdam 1 May keys decrypt other U534 traffic, **not** this Thetis message — shown by exhaustion, not assumption.
- Online archival levers previously spent: no depth, no kiss, no recovered key sheet, indicators ≈ 0.94 bit. **Archive work is no longer idle**: the Wenselaers team arm has live enquiries for original Baltic training radio/key papers, a second Thetis message, and allocation-list provenance (Phase 55). As of 2026-09-25 evening, nothing new is in hand: no second text, no A-hit, 1772a and 2499 still unconnected. Eric Reits (Duivendrecht) has been asked for everything registered to the same acquisition as Quelle 2499. Paul's recollection that the set came from Norway, and that it arrived with the Funkverkehrsheft and Der Schlüssel M, stays his recollection. U-2529 was an Ausbildungsboot in Horten 30 April–2 May; requests are out to Nasjonalarkivet, Marinemuseet, the RM45 III-120 KTB, and the U-977 Reel 84 material. That is a receiver-side archive in the window, not an identification of Thetis or of this message.
- **M-Thetis is the working assignment, with an allocation-list chronology caveat.** `ACH` maps to Thetis under the later list used by Hörenberg; an earlier reported `Forelle` list maps column 645 to Aegir. List succession plausibly resolves that without changing the K-Buch column, but the exact later-list date/codename/provenance is pending. The intended recipient is also open: preservation among U-534 papers does not prove the signal was addressed to U-534.
- Exact ≥16 × rings AAAA is a clean negative **under the head gate** too (Phase 14): 868 menus, 3 physical, 0 breaks. The mid-message divergence hypothesis does not hide a key in the turnover-free slice.
- Catalog ring sweep through originalIndex 417/2513 is the full-order receipt (Phase 15). The VIII-fast continuation is **RUNNING** from 418 (2026-09-25T14:47:56Z): 451 menus after the 16-letter filter, 42 of 336 orders, log `logs/campaign-catalog-rings-viii-fast.log`. A dry pass is not wheel-order coverage.
- Full middle × right ring coverage under `--subspace viii-fast-wheel` is **DONE against P1030680, clean negative** (Phase 58): placement 1 dead over 4.152×10¹¹ settings at full 336 WO; placements 2–24 **23/23 dead, 0 raw stops over 1.194×10¹² settings**, 0 quarantine. This is VIII-fast subspace coverage (42 of 336 orders), not wheel-order coverage. The covered-lane skip is graded (0 violations); the blind control breaks at full middle × right coverage in 4,307 s at 96.4M/s.
- **The two highest-risk 28–40-letter re-runs are complete; the remaining 300 catalog placements are deprioritised.** The old Metal `MAX_UPPER=4` cap skipped up to 5.56% of lanes at length 40, 1.14% at 30, and 0.29% at 28 (0% at ≤25). On the corrected kernel, the Phase 16 body arm is **40/40 dead with 0 raw stops**, and the Phase 8 top-30 arm reproduces the same 73 raw stops over 4.79×10¹¹ settings with zero additional survivors and no break. Those two maximum-contamination receipts re-establish their negatives. The 300-place remainder is retained but parked; strongest examples are included in the partial full-middle-ring arm. Phase 23's Regenbogen queue and every 16–25-letter negative are unaffected.
- Fuzzed × AAAA is a clean negative (256k raw stops, 0 physical); the fuzzed ring sweep on the curated top-40 is also a **clean negative** (0 raw stops across all 40).
- **Übung hypothesis (Phase 16):** ≥16 training headers @0–2 AAAA = clean negative (0 physical). Mid-message body anchors all dead under AAAA **and** under full right-ring sweep (40/40, 0 raw stops) — the “header pushes the body back” claim is exhausted for these cribs (middle ring still pinned A). Short-menu “clears the bar” lines were prefix flukes.
- **Thetis register (Phases 17, 68, and 71):** Kenngruppe drill + training openings @0–5 × AAAA = clean negative (3 physical, 0 breaks). Right-ring sweep **done, 73/73, no break** (2026-09-25T04:59:39Z). Menus 66–73: best IC 0.038, tail −4.629. Three `FUNKSPRUCHUEBUNG` quarantine rows are prefix hits; every whole-message tail misses −4.000 (−5.256, −4.534, −4.733). Not an escalate. Next morning: catalog 418–2513 under `--subspace viii-fast-wheel`, log `logs/campaign-catalog-rings-viii-fast.log`. Middle ring stays A. Menu 62 finished its pinned-A pass before the halt: 84 raw stops, 7 physical. Shell UKW C / beta / VI-I-II / rings AAAJ / pos MWEW / stecker AP BG CO DK EU HJ IT MR VY (9). Whole-message IC 0.049 and tail −4.387 miss the bar (floor 0.055, tail > −3.600). Head letters 0..32: IC 0.058, tail −3.416. Printed text `ESCHULFUNKSPRUCHXHEHQDASDIDESSJUQMTWMQHIBEMDEUOLFULRIPLCHHSIUZZSQAHPNZTE`. Not a full plaintext. The one-menu middle-ring sweep is done: 343 raw stops, 4 physical, same AAAJ head. Locked nine plugs: best IC 0.044, tail −3.710, below the bar. A head-only hit now prints and the sweep continues. Log `logs/campaign-thetis-register-rings.log`.
- **Crib-free path (Phases 50 and 54.4):** the Ostwald/Weierud ciphertext-only attack remains negative at 72 letters, but the largest lever is now reverified under the repaired dense scorer. Unseeded generic is 5/30, median −0.2306. Single-plug exhaust-6 reaches **−0.0672** (~71% of the deficit removed) and saturates by exhaust-10 at −0.0567. New two-plug exhaustion reaches **30% / −0.0361** at exhaust-6; widening to exhaust-10 yields 40% but a worse median **−0.0486**, so it also saturates. Controls are thin, the margin never crosses zero, and no crib-free break is claimed. Four oracle-correct plugs still flip the sign, but that is an upper bound on finishing a true stop, not finding one.
- **Tolerant diagonal board / legacy Mulein arm (Phase 51):** the board counts contradictions and accepts a setting when deleting ≤ *t* menu edges makes it close. On a known key, 1–3 corrupted crib letters make the exact board lose the true setting while the matching tolerant board keeps it with 25/25 plug deductions correct; Metal agrees with host at **0/192 lane mismatches**. Inflation is placement-dependent, so the withdrawn crib-length rule stays withdrawn. Against P1030680, tolerance 1 × right rings is **SUSPENDED at 3/24**: all three completed strongest placements are dead at the board; resume `--bombe-menus 0 --bombe-ring-sweep --bombe-garble-tolerance 1 --bombe-from 4`. This is three local negatives, not a garble verdict.
- **Post-gap indel / Nazi Blaster 9000 (Phases 51.12–53.6):** the legacy exact-board post-gap δ=4 run is **SUSPENDED at 144/237**; entries 1–110 and 112–144 are dead, while entry 111's 8 raw stops produced 0 valid ≤10-plug completions. Resume `--bombe-menus 0 --bombe-from 145`; middle ring remains A. Separately, the production Verilog bank passes full held-receipt parity and selected `BANK_LANES=4` at 276.035 receipts/s. Its bounded operational stripe records 10,048/10,048 chunks, 4,163,640 receipts, 4,184 host-verified physical candidates, and 0 BREAK gates. The read-only adjudicator has now closed every **exact** candidate: all 40 identity and all 41 post-gap exact/no-drop hits die at joint ≤10-plug completion; all **81 are split-menu ghosts**. Identity-family repairs with ≥4 forced plugs were consumed by `--mulein-ostwald-adapt` / `--ostwald-escalate` (Phases 62–63): **38 then 824 climbed, NO BREAK**, below the random-setting floor; 1,232 identity repairs remain below the pair floor; post-gap repairs remain unclimbed (gapped walk built, not consumed). Settings `256..<456976`, other shells, and the printed legacy boundaries remain open. Cleartext Float TensorLUT/Metal, not FHE or encrypted tick rate; no key, plaintext, or decrypt.
- **Archive round-trip failures are real but mostly not garble (Phase 50.9 — corrects 50.1):** 17 of 48 published keys do not reproduce their own recorded plaintext, and the first write-up called that archive garble. Withdrawn. Most are multi-part messages with per-part message keys (the >320-letter ones), where end-to-end decryption under a single key correctly yields a good head and a garbage tail. Only **4 controls, 8 events**, are isolated substitutions. The garble hypothesis for P1030680 still stands on Girard's documented degarbling of the sister message — not on this.
- **Stochastic Bombe (Phases 18–20):** GPU letter-match live. Control oracle/near-miss
  PASS. Training, collapse, weather/keyboard, meta-evolve, and shell-RIGA+free rings
  all **0 survivors** under rigor (ceiling ~60–69% = coincidence). Wrong prior + more
  hardware ≠ break. Catalog right-ring sweep parked at 417; resume from 418. Option B parked.
  `stochastic-bombe.md`.
- **TensorLUT (Phase 21):** Yosys→64-wide continuous INIT→Metal stream→Verilog loop
  closed on `enigma_m4`. Baseline \(F_{crypto}=0\) locked. Full wipe and stecker-cone
  INIT melts **shatter** under λ (dependency shortcuts; cone LUTs are I/O codecs around
  identity `plugboard()`, not reciprocal pairs). Adaptation: freeze the core; evolve a
  `SteckerInvolution` sandwich — identity control \(F=0\). Not a P1030680 break claim.
  `adversarial-synthesis.md`, `tensorlut.md`.
- **Near-miss quarantine (Phase 22):** Soft-band Welchman stops (IC≥0.048, tail>−4.0,
  crib exact, below BREAK) dump to `logs/quarantine_candidates.json` and seed Stochastic
  via `--hybrid-quarantine`. Graded on synthetic P1030684 block-wipe **and** historical
  P1030681 first-draft soft trap (334/372). Soft-tail −4.3 UEBUNG archaeology: 55
  quarantine seeds, Hybrid escalate 0 survivors. Exact-menu clean negatives do not rule
  out a mis-read ciphertext.
- **Regenbogen / Hannibal (Phase 23):** Under right-ring coverage (middle ring pinned A),
  the ciphertext is **not** `BEFEHLERHALTENREGENBOGEN` (0 stops AAAA+rings), **not** the
  five scuttle phrases as a BREAK (soft escalate 0/~58%), and **not** the Hela/
  Ostpreussen evacuation phrases as a BREAK (soft escalate 0/~54%). Soft-band physicals
  exist; locked-shell stecker wiggle does not. Own-orders AAAA: 0 physical; filler AAAA:
  below soft — no ring sweeps warranted. Queue closed; Metal returned to catalog rings.
- Short cribs under confirm≥2 no longer GPU-sweep; they only host-retest shells a ≥16 anchor locked. Solo BREAK for cribs <16 is gated.
- Every crib in the original catalog was mined from Potsdam/second/third-net traffic. **No broken Thetis message exists in the corpus** (Phase 13); UEBUNG and Thetis-register were the first Thetis-specific wedges.
- **The Wenselaers archival arm is now part of the campaign team (Phases 52 and 55).** Selm Merel Wenselaers (Historian / Curator, Amsterdam / Antwerp) is a deeply valued contributor leading the historical/archival work alongside HELUT's mechanical cryptanalysis. Her source research defines evidence-backed questions; the graded toolchain turns them into controlled measurements; the resulting findings are joint campaign work. Her first three phrases are clean negatives under exact and post-gap δ=4 right-ring coverage (28/28, 0 raw stops in each arm). Her new tranche establishes one additional **repo-verbatim** radio phrase — `FFFTTTBLEIBTBESETZT` in P1030668 — while the corrupted P1030707 sister and clean boat-number reconstructions remain printed as corruption/withdrawal. Named DEFE/KTB/allocation-list findings are source-reported pending receipts; they link Neustadt transfer with scuttling, open intended-recipient and scheduled-broadcast hypotheses, and qualify the later Thetis allocation-list provenance without reopening eliminated Potsdam keys. The exact 40-letter P1030668 context is **DONE** as four 18-loop single-component placements under `--subspace viii-fast-wheel --bombe-middle-ring --bombe-ring-sweep` (Phase 60; log `logs/campaign-wenselaers-bleibt-besetzt-viii-fast-middlering.log`): **4/4 dead, 0 raw stops over 2.076×10¹¹ settings**, 0 quarantine. Scope is 42 of 336 wheel orders. Phase 61: NARA RG 457 key-log request is acknowledged with no box read; RIP 401 non-random wheel orders are not a cross-net answer; NID 1/PW/REP/17 reconstructs Baltic working-up and W/T listening practice but **never names Thetis**; 3 May WEGA/OTTO WÜNSCHE Kenngruppen are the designed natural experiment and are not in hand. Phase 61.2: Bauer cites Anhang F for Hydra May 1945 Steckerverbindungen (in use through 7 May); the appendix page is not in the chapter extract and is not a Thetis key. VIII-fast stays an ordering prior. No new GPU.
- Menu strength is **connectivity**, not just edge count.
- Enigma@Home appears defunct; share status with Hörenberg / fellow workers, not a dead BOINC project.
- Success = naval German plaintext + ≤10-plug board, crib exact, IC ≥ 0.055, trigram tail > −3.600 — whole message **or** a head of ≥16 non-crib letters — after contradiction-free SAT deduction.

*When this message breaks — or when a defined attack is fully exhausted — amend this journal with the date, the key, and the plaintext. Until then, every clean negative is part of the record.*
